### 4. 📋 待办（条件触发式）
- **mvp2_eval_lr5e6_r1_ckpt20 完成** → 看 rel_f1：≥0.30 = P0 PASS（lr=5e-6 验证成功，铺后续 eval 矩阵）；<0.30 = 需要更低 lr 或其他修复，产 Framing 提案给 Director

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
- **核心发现（D024）**: CED reward 不仅改善 evidence quality，还通过 reward variance 防止 GRPO mode collapse。这是论文最重要的 finding——CED 兼具双重作用。extraction-only 和 flat-NLI reward 在相同配置下都 collapse，只有 CED 存活。
- **lr=5e-5 全灭原因（D029）**: 灾难性遗忘 + CED recall shortcut（少预测→高 CED→recall 崩塌）。lr=5e-6 是当前唯一活路。
- **CED recall shortcut 已修复（D032）**: `--ced_recall_penalty` flag 乘以 recall factor 惩罚 under-prediction。P0+P1 实验测试此修复。
- **决策链**: D008(KL不兼容) → D020(kl=0.01配置) → D024(CED anti-collapse) → D027/D029(lr过高) → D032(recall shortcut) → 当前 lr=5e-6 实验
- **易错点**:
  - PREPARING 里的 kl001_r2/r3 是旧 lr=5e-5 配置的 retry，**不要提交**——它们的前提假设（lr=5e-5 可行）已被 D029 推翻
  - eval 指标要看 rel_f1（相对 SFT baseline），不是 raw F1——SFT baseline F1=0.4057
  - report.md 的"资源状态"显示全部空闲是 auto-generated 的 bug，实际 3 卡在跑
  - wandb MCP 工具 `list_wandb_runs` 有 bug，用 `fetch_wandb_run_history` + run_id 代替

## 项目硬约束 (memory.md 节选)

## 环境与兼容性

- **AutoDL HuggingFace 下载不可靠**：大模型（如 Qwen3-4B）从 HF 下载反复 ReadTimeout。解决方案：用 ModelScope (`from modelscope import snapshot_download`) 下载到本地缓存，再用本地路径加载。(2026-05-14)
- **vLLM 版本不兼容 TRL**：服务器预装 vLLM 0.18.1，但 TRL 支持范围为 0.11.0-0.17.1。MVP-2 若用 TRL 内部 vLLM rollout 可能有兼容问题，需提前测试。(2026-05-14)
- **flash_attn 未安装**：rtx5090-4x 服务器未预装 flash-attn，训练脚本需用 `attn_implementation="sdpa"` 替代。(2026-05-14)
- **Qwen3-4B 本地路径**：已通过 ModelScope 下载到 `/root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen3-4B`（7.6GB）。后续训练脚本应指向此路径而非 HF model ID。(2026-05-14)
- **确认训练配置**：ZeRO-2 + QLoRA (4-bit NF4, rank 64, alpha 128) + SDPA。ZeRO-3 + QLoRA 不兼容（uint8 vs all_gather dtype）。(2026-05-14)
- **Smoke test 假阴性**：synthetic data reward variance=0 导致零梯度是数学必然（format reward 对所有 completions 输出相同分数），不是训练 bug。评估 smoke test 时只看兼容性和显存，不看 loss 趋势。(2026-05-14)
- **TRL 1.1.0 API 变更（关键）**：服务器 TRL 版本 1.1.0，与旧版有 3 个破坏性变更：(1) `SFTConfig.max_seq_length` → `max_length`；(2) `SFTTrainer(tokenizer=...)` → `SFTTrainer(processing_class=...)`；(3) 多 GPU 需要 accelerate `MULTI_GPU` + `device_map={"": local_rank}`。**grpo_trainer.py 也用了 `tokenizer=`，GRPO 提交前必须改为 `processing_class=`**。(2026-05-14)
- **rtx5090-4x 和 gpu-5090-4x-1 是同一台机器**：两个 SSH endpoint 指向同一台 4×RTX 5090 32GB 服务器。(2026-05-14)
- **wandb 配置**：entity=`xiaoyucorynne-`，GRPO 项目名=`freige-grpo`（但截至 2026-05-14 项目未成功创建——训练脚本可能未连接 wandb）。API key 存于 `~/.netrc`。MCP 工具 `list_wandb_runs` 有内部 bug（`wandb_project` 参数传递错误），需直接用 `fetch_wandb_run_history` + 已知 run_id。(2026-05-14)

```json
{
  "parentUuid": "4b0944df-792f-4f9b-abfb-292816db9cfc",

  "attachment": {
    "type": "skill_listing",
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",
```

```text
Excellent! Two critical findings:

1. **cuda:2 already has a task** — the `worker_ops_launch-ckpt40-eval-verify-gpus` worker launched a ckpt-40 eval on cuda:2 (PID 100549/100555). But this was from the previous loop, and the cron reconcile didn't pick it up. Let me check if there's a corresponding registry entry for this eval.

2. **kl001_r4 multi-eval diagnosis**: The process was externally killed after completing ckpt-30 eval. 

Looking at the F1 scores across six checkpoints, there's an upward trend with ckpt-90 reaching 0.211, but that's still concerning—the learning rate of 5e-5 appears problematic. When I compare this to the SFT baseline of 0.4057, the relative F1 is only around 0.52, which technically exceeds the 0.30 threshold but the early checkpoints show severe degradation starting from ckpt-10 with an F1 of just 0.003.

The pattern is clear: catastrophic forgetting happened early in training and only partially recovered by checkpoint 90, never approaching the baseline performance. Now I need to check whether the ckpt-40 evaluation from the previous loop is still running—the worker showed PIDs 100549/100555, but the cron reconcile didn't flag it and there's no registry entry tracking it.

Looking at the current RUNNING experiments in the registry, I see training jobs on cuda:0 and cuda:3, plus an eval on cuda:1, but no ckpt-40 eval listed. That means the previous loop likely spawned an orphan process on cuda:2 that's been running unmonitored. I need to spawn a worker to check if that process is still alive.

The launch worker finished at 21:12 UTC, and if the ckpt-40 eval takes around 2.5 hours like the ckpt-20 one did, it should still be running. But the report.md marks cuda:2 as idle, which conflicts with what the worker actually launched — the report was probably auto-generated before the eval started or the eval just wasn't registered in the system.

I need to check if the ckpt-40 eval is still alive on cuda:2, set up proper monitoring if it is, or collect results if it's finished. I'll spawn a worker to check the process status on rtx5090-4x and look at the GPU usage, then check the log file if the process has already terminated. I should also review the resource-saturation skill before reporting back.

But I'm realizing the worker pool is currently empty, and the protocol requires at least 2 workers running at all times. So I need to spawn multiple workers in parallel — one to check the ckpt-40 eval status and another to handle something useful like preparing for upcoming evaluations or doing literature research. Let me get the full picture of what's happening with ckpt-40 first, then decide on the second worker's task. ckpt-40 eval is running on cuda:2 from the previous loop but I don't have visibility into its status since there's no cron monitoring. The kl001_r4 multi-eval experiment was killed externally, though it did complete 6 checkpoint evaluations before that—the best F1 score of 0.211 confirms that the lr=5e-5 setting causes catastrophic forgetting. My worker pool is empty, so I need to spawn new workers and then report the current state to Director.
```

````json
{
  "task_type": "ops:check-cuda2-ckpt40-eval",
  "project": "faith-evidence-ie",
  "task_prompt": "## 任务\n检查 cuda:2 上的 ckpt-40 eval 进程状态。\n\n## 背景\n上一轮 Agent 在 rtx5090-4x 的 cuda:2 上启动了 P0 ckpt-40 eval（PID 100549，子进程 PID 100555），但未通过 submit_training_job 注册 cron 监控。需要确认进程是否存活、是否已完成、结果如何。\n\n## 步骤\n1. **SSH 到 rtx5090-4x**（ssh_read_only），执行：\n   ```\n   ps -p 100549 -o pid,stat,etime,cmd 2>/dev/null; ps -p 100555 -o pid,stat,etime,cmd 2>/dev/null; echo \"---\"; nvidia-smi -i 2 --query-compute-apps=pid,used_memory --format=csv,noheader 2>/dev/null; echo \"---\"; tail -30 /tmp/eval_lr5e6_ckpt40.log 2>/dev/null || echo \"NO_LOG\"\n   ```\n\n2. **判断状态**：\n   - 进程活着 + GPU 在用 → 报告 \"ALIVE\"，附当前进度\n   - 进程死了 + log 有完整结果 → 报告 \"DONE\"，提取 metrics（F1, precision, recall, evi-f1, edcr, pred_count）\n   - 进程死了 + log 无完整结果 → 报告 \"FAILED\"，附最后 30 行 log\n\n3. **额外检查**：P0 训练（PID 90080/90085，cuda:0）当前到了第几步？\n   ```\n   tail -5 /root/autodl-tmp/grpo_ced_lr5e6/training.log 2>/dev/null || ls -la /root/autodl-tmp/grpo_ced_lr5e6/checkpoint-* 2>/dev/null | tail -5\n   ```\n\n## 服务器\n- SSH endpoint: rtx5090-4x\n- conda 环境: `source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`\n- 工作目录: /root/autodl-tmp/freige\n\n## 产出\n简洁报告：cuda:2 eval 状态 + P0 训练进度。",
  "tool_groups": [
    "ssh"
  ]
}
````

```text
3	description: "当你准备向 Director 汇报前、收到 cron/worker 回调、审批通过准备开跑、怀疑 Worker 池不够饱和时,立即使用本 skill。四部分:A) GPU 分配率(分配了没跑=失职) B) GPU 效率 C) 非 GPU 任务并行(Worker 池<2=失职) D) 汇报必须带 [资源] 行。"
4	always: false
5	roles:
6	  - director
7	  - agent
8	---
9	
10	# 资源饱和度自检（硬约束）
11	
12	## A. GPU 分配率检查
13	
14	调 `get_project_resource_usage(project)` 看:
15	- 已分配给本项目的 GPU 总数和具体卡号
16	- 实际在跑任务的 GPU 数和具体卡号（交叉 registry RUNNING + nvidia-smi 进程）
17	- 分配了但没跑的 GPU 清单 ⚠️
18	
19	任何"分配了但没跑"的 GPU = 失职。必须立即:
20	- 派 Worker 铺 dry-run / 下一批实验代码准备
21	- 铺 ablation（从 experiment-matrix 空白格子里挑）
22	- 确实无实验可铺 → 向 Director 汇报"暂无可铺实验"+ 建议下一步方向(扩 ablation 维度 / 加 baseline / 加 benchmark)。**不要建议归还 GPU**——项目级 GPU 归还只在归档时由 `archive_project` 自动触发, 不是 Director 中途的动作
23	
24	**单实验不占多卡**: 分配 4 卡但实验只用 1 卡 → 另 3 卡必须并行跑其他实验,不能空占。
25	
26	## B. GPU 效率检查
27	
28	调 `get_gpu_health(server)`:
29	- 空闲 GPU（util < 10% 持续 > 5 min）→ 立即铺任务
30	- 低利用率（util < 50% 持续 > 15 min）→ spawn_worker 诊断（batch / IO / 同步）
31	- OOM 风险（显存 > 90% 持续 10 min）→ 主动降 batch / 调优
32	
33	## C. 非 GPU 任务并行
34	
35	GPU 在跑 ≠ 你空闲。以下 CPU / 网络任务**必须**派独立 Worker 并行:
36	- 下一批实验的数据集下载 / 预处理 / fingerprint
37	- 下一批实验的代码准备 / dry-run / 环境配置
38	- 论文文献调研 / related work 检索
39	- 已完成实验的 log 深度分析 / metric 聚合
40	- 论文章节起草
41	- 代码 review / refactor
42	
43	**硬约束**: 任一时刻 Worker 池 < 2 个 = 失职（除非真的全部下游阻塞在等 GPU,这时应当向 Director 申请扩 GPU）。
44	
45	## D. 汇报格式
46	
47	向 Director 汇报时开头必须带资源声明行:
48	
49	    [资源] GPU 分配 N / 实际在跑 M | Worker 池 K (其中 GPU 任务 A / CPU 任务 B)
50	
```

````json
{
  "task_type": "analysis:wandb-training-progress",
  "project": "faith-evidence-ie",
  "task_prompt": "## 任务\n查询两个正在运行的 GRPO 训练实验的 wandb 指标，评估训练趋势。\n\n## 背景\n当前两个 GRPO 训练在 rtx5090-4x 上运行：\n- **P0** (mvp2_grpo_ced_lr5e6_r1): lr=5e-6 纯 CED reward，wandb run 可能叫 \"grpo-s3-ced-lr5e6-r1\"\n- **P0+P1** (mvp2_grpo_ced_lr5e6_rp_r2): lr=5e-6 + recall_penalty，wandb run 可能叫 \"grpo-s3-ced-lr5e6-rp-r2\"\n\nwandb 配置: entity=\"xiaoyucorynne-\", project=\"freige-grpo\"\n\n**注意**: `list_wandb_runs` 工具有 bug（wandb_project 参数传递错误）。请直接用 `fetch_wandb_metrics` 并尝试不同的 run_id 格式（如完整 run name、或从 wandb API 获取）。\n\n## 步骤\n1. 尝试用 `fetch_wandb_metrics` 拉取两个 run 的指标。如果 run_id 不对，试用 SSH 到服务器查看 wandb 日志：\n   ```\n   ls /root/autodl-tmp/grpo_ced_lr5e6/wandb/latest-run/ 2>/dev/null\n   cat /root/autodl-tmp/grpo_ced_lr5e6/wandb/latest-run/run-*.wandb 2>/dev/null | head -5\n   ```\n   或者查看 wandb run ID：\n   ```\n   grep -r \"wandb\" /root/autodl-tmp/grpo_ced_lr5e6/*.log 2>/dev/null | head -5\n   ```\n\n2. 关键指标（如果 wandb 可用）：\n   - `reward/ced_reward_mean` — CED reward 趋势\n   - `reward/f1_reward_mean` — F1 reward 趋势\n   - `reward/total_reward_mean` — 总 reward\n   - `train/loss` — 训练 loss\n   - `train/grad_norm` — 梯度范数\n   - `eval/*` — 如果有 eval metrics\n\n3. 如果 wandb 不可用，从训练 log 提取关键指标：\n   ```\n   tail -50 /root/autodl-tmp/grpo_ced_lr5e6/training.log 2>/dev/null\n   # 或者找到 TRL 的标准输出\n   grep -E \"step|reward|loss\" /root/runs/faith-evidence-ie/mvp2_grpo_ced_lr5e6_r1.log 2>/dev/null | tail -20\n   ```\n\n## 服务器\n- SSH: rtx5090-4x\n- conda: `source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`\n\n## 产出\n表格式汇报每个 run 的当前 step 和关键指标趋势（上升/下降/平稳）。特别关注：\n- reward 是否在上升（lr=5e-6 验证关键信号）\n- 是否有 mode collapse 迹象（reward 方差 → 0）\n- pred_count / recall 趋势（CED recall shortcut 检测）",
  "tool_groups": [
    "ssh",
    "wandb"
  ]
}
````
